  &lt;/section&gt;

  &lt;footer&gt;
    Aeon Console is deliberately quiet until you attach real JSON. The Garden stays sovereign; tooling may observe but never own it.
  &lt;/footer&gt;
&lt;/div&gt;

&lt;script&gt;
  // =========================
  // Aeon Dashboard Loader v2
  // Case-tolerant + schema-tolerant + cache-busting
  // =========================

  function byId(id) { return document.getElementById(id); }

  function setText(id, text) {
    var el = byId(id);
    if (el) el.textContent = (text === undefined || text === null) ? &quot;&quot; : String(text);
  }

  function setStatusPill(pillId, mode, textId, text) {
    var pill = byId(pillId);
    if (!pill) return;
    pill.classList.remove(&quot;ok&quot;, &quot;warn&quot;, &quot;err&quot;);
    if (mode) pill.classList.add(mode);
    setText(textId, text);
  }

  function showError(id, msg) {
    var el = byId(id);
    if (!el) return;
    el.style.display = &quot;block&quot;;
    el.textContent = msg;
  }

  function hideError(id) {
    var el = byId(id);
    if (!el) return;
    el.style.display = &quot;none&quot;;
    el.textContent = &quot;&quot;;
  }

  async function tryFetchJson(paths) {
    var list = Array.isArray(paths) ? paths : [paths];
    var lastErr = null;

    for (var i = 0; i &lt; list.length; i++) {
      var p = list[i];
      try {
        // cache-bust to avoid GitHub Pages serving stale JSON
        var url = p + (p.indexOf(&quot;?&quot;) &gt;= 0 ? &quot;&amp;&quot; : &quot;?&quot;) + &quot;v=&quot; + Date.now();
        var res = await fetch(url, { cache: &quot;no-store&quot; });
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error(&quot;HTTP &quot; + res.status + &quot; for &quot; + p);
        return await res.json();
      } catch (e) {
        lastErr = e;
      }
    }

    throw (lastErr || new Error(&quot;All JSON paths failed&quot;));
  }

  // --------- Helpers to normalize common schema shapes ---------

  function firstTruthy() {
    for (var i = 0; i &lt; arguments.length; i++) {
      if (arguments[i]) return arguments[i];
    }
    return null;
  }

  function asArray(v) { return Array.isArray(v) ? v : []; }

  function safeNumber(v, fallback) {
    return (typeof v === &quot;number&quot; &amp;&amp; isFinite(v)) ? v : fallback;
  }

  // --------- Loaders ---------

  async function loadStatus() {
    hideError(&quot;status-error&quot;);
    try {
      var data = await tryFetchJson([&quot;STATUS.json&quot;, &quot;status.json&quot;]);
      var phase = firstTruthy(data.phase, data.garden_phase, data.state &amp;&amp; data.state.phase, &quot;–&quot;);
      var health = firstTruthy(data.health, data.garden_health, data.state &amp;&amp; data.state.health, &quot;–&quot;);
      var echoes = safeNumber(
        (data.counts &amp;&amp; data.counts.echoes),
        safeNumber(data.echo_count, safeNumber(data.echoes, 0))
      );

      setText(&quot;status-phase&quot;, phase);
      setText(&quot;status-health&quot;, health);
      setText(&quot;status-echoes&quot;, echoes);

      setText(&quot;status-phase-sub&quot;, &quot;Loaded from STATUS.json.&quot;);
      setText(&quot;status-health-sub&quot;, &quot;Loaded from STATUS.json.&quot;);
      setText(&quot;status-echoes-sub&quot;, &quot;Counts pulled from STATUS.json.&quot;);

      setStatusPill(&quot;status-json-pill&quot;, &quot;ok&quot;, &quot;status-json-text&quot;, &quot;STATUS.json loaded&quot;);
      setText(&quot;status-note&quot;, &quot;STATUS.json present. Aeon Console will reflect the Garden snapshot.&quot;);
      setText(&quot;status-file-pill&quot;, &quot;Path: ./STATUS.json&quot;);
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn(&quot;STATUS.json optional:&quot;, err);
      setStatusPill(&quot;status-json-pill&quot;, &quot;warn&quot;, &quot;status-json-text&quot;, &quot;STATUS.json missing&quot;);
      setText(&quot;status-note&quot;, &quot;STATUS.json is optional for now. This section will light up when you wire it.&quot;);
      setText(&quot;status-file-pill&quot;, &quot;Path: ./STATUS.json&quot;);
      // keep defaults; do not show as hard error
    }
  }

  async function loadEchoIndex() {
    hideError(&quot;echo-error&quot;);
    try {
      var data = await tryFetchJson([&quot;machine-index.json&quot;, &quot;MACHINE-INDEX.json&quot;, &quot;MACHINE-INDEX.JSON&quot;, &quot;machine-index-beta.json&quot;]);

      // Support both:
      // A) { entries:[...] } or [...]
      // B) { counts, cycles_manifest:{cycles:[{echoes:[{file,title,timestamp,...}]}]}, echoes_feed_header:[{title,date,...}] }
      var entries = [];
      if (data &amp;&amp; Array.isArray(data.entries)) {
        entries = data.entries;
      } else if (Array.isArray(data)) {
        entries = data;
      } else if (data &amp;&amp; data.cycles_manifest &amp;&amp; Array.isArray(data.cycles_manifest.cycles)) {
        data.cycles_manifest.cycles.forEach(function (cycle) {
          asArray(cycle.echoes).forEach(function (e) {
            entries.push({
              file: e.file || e.path || &quot;&quot;,
              title: e.title || e.slug || &quot;&quot;,
              timestamp: e.timestamp || e.time || e.ts || null
            });
          });
        });
      }

      var tracked = (data &amp;&amp; data.counts) ? safeNumber(data.counts.echoes, entries.length) : entries.length;

      setText(&quot;echo-count&quot;, tracked);
      setText(&quot;echo-count-sub&quot;, tracked &gt; 0 ? &quot;machine-index.json present.&quot; : &quot;Index loaded, but no Echo entries found.&quot;);

      // Directories (best-effort) from file prefix
      var byDir = {};
      entries.forEach(function (entry) {
        var path = entry.path || entry.file || &quot;&quot;;
        if (!path) path = &quot;&quot;;
        var parts = path.split(&quot;/&quot;);
        var dir = parts.length &gt; 1 ? parts[0] : &quot;(root)&quot;;
        byDir[dir] = (byDir[dir] || 0) + 1;
      });

      var dirKeys = Object.keys(byDir);
      setText(&quot;echo-dirs&quot;, dirKeys.length ? dirKeys.length : &quot;–&quot;);
      setText(
        &quot;echo-dirs-sub&quot;,
        dirKeys.length
          ? (&quot;Directories with Echo entries: &quot; + dirKeys.slice(0, 3).join(&quot;, &quot;) + (dirKeys.length &gt; 3 ? &quot;…&quot; : &quot;&quot;))
          : &quot;No Echo directories found in index.&quot;
      );

      // Latest (prefer echoes_feed_header)
      var latestLabel = &quot;–&quot;;
      if (data &amp;&amp; Array.isArray(data.echoes_feed_header) &amp;&amp; data.echoes_feed_header.length) {
        var header = data.echoes_feed_header
          .filter(function (x) { return x &amp;&amp; x.date; })
          .sort(function (a, b) { return String(b.date).localeCompare(String(a.date)); })[0];
        if (header) latestLabel = header.title || header.file || header.slug || header.date || &quot;–&quot;;
      } else {
        // fall back: highest timestamp-like string
        var latestTs = null;
        var latest = null;
        entries.forEach(function (entry) {
          var ts = entry.timestamp || null;
          if (ts &amp;&amp; (!latestTs || String(ts) &gt; String(latestTs))) {
            latestTs = ts;
            latest = entry;
          }
        });
        if (latest) latestLabel = latest.title || latest.file || latest.path || &quot;–&quot;;
      }

      setText(&quot;echo-latest&quot;, latestLabel);
      setText(&quot;echo-latest-sub&quot;, &quot;Latest entry from machine index (best-effort).&quot;);

      setStatusPill(&quot;echo-json-pill&quot;, &quot;ok&quot;, &quot;echo-json-text&quot;, &quot;machine-index.json loaded&quot;);
      setText(&quot;echo-note&quot;, &quot;machine-index.json is present. This feed is for machines while humans stay in the Codex.&quot;);
      setText(&quot;echo-file-pill&quot;, &quot;Path: ./MACHINE-INDEX.JSON&quot;);
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn(&quot;machine-index.json optional:&quot;, err);
      setStatusPill(&quot;echo-json-pill&quot;, &quot;warn&quot;, &quot;echo-json-text&quot;, &quot;machine-index.json missing or invalid&quot;);
      setText(&quot;echo-note&quot;, &quot;machine-index.json is optional. Echo counts fall back to manual reading until it appears.&quot;);
      showError(&quot;echo-error&quot;, &quot;machine-index.json not found or could not be parsed. Safe to ignore until you&#x27;re ready.&quot;);
    }
  }

  async function loadScanReport() {
    hideError(&quot;scan-error&quot;);
    try {
      var data = await tryFetchJson([&quot;garden_scan_report.json&quot;, &quot;GARDEN_SCAN_REPORT.json&quot;]);

      // Support common shapes:
      // A) { files_with_hits, total_hits, top_pattern }
      // B) { filesWithHits, totalHits, topPattern }
      // C) { hits:[...], patterns:[...] } (derive)
      var filesWithHits = safeNumber(
        firstTruthy(data.files_with_hits, data.filesWithHits, data.files_with_matches, data.file_count_with_hits),
        null
      );
      var totalHits = safeNumber(firstTruthy(data.total_hits, data.totalHits, data.hit_count), null);
      var topPattern = firstTruthy(data.top_pattern, data.topPattern, data.most_common, null);

      if (filesWithHits === null &amp;&amp; Array.isArray(data.hits)) filesWithHits = data.hits.length;
      if (totalHits === null &amp;&amp; Array.isArray(data.hits)) totalHits = data.hits.reduce(function (a, h) { return a + safeNumber(h.count, 0); }, 0);
      if (!topPattern &amp;&amp; Array.isArray(data.patterns) &amp;&amp; data.patterns.length) topPattern = data.patterns[0].pattern || data.patterns[0].name || &quot;none&quot;;

      if (filesWithHits === null) filesWithHits = 0;
      if (totalHits === null) totalHits = 0;
      if (!topPattern) topPattern = &quot;none&quot;;

      setText(&quot;scan-files&quot;, filesWithHits);
      setText(&quot;scan-total&quot;, totalHits);
      setText(&quot;scan-pattern&quot;, topPattern);

      setText(&quot;scan-files-sub&quot;, filesWithHits ? &quot;Files containing Garden signatures.&quot; : &quot;No signature hits or report is empty.&quot;);
      setText(&quot;scan-total-sub&quot;, totalHits ? &quot;Pattern matches recorded.&quot; : &quot;No pattern matches recorded.&quot;);
      setText(&quot;scan-pattern-sub&quot;, &quot;Most frequently observed signature across the repo.&quot;);

      setStatusPill(&quot;scan-json-pill&quot;, &quot;ok&quot;, &quot;scan-json-text&quot;, &quot;garden_scan_report.json loaded&quot;);
      setText(&quot;scan-note&quot;, &quot;garden_scan_report.json present. Signature scan results are now visible.&quot;);
      setText(&quot;scan-file-pill&quot;, &quot;Path: ./GARDEN_SCAN_REPORT.JSON&quot;);
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn(&quot;scan report optional:&quot;, err);
      setStatusPill(&quot;scan-json-pill&quot;, &quot;warn&quot;, &quot;scan-json-text&quot;, &quot;garden_scan_report.json missing or invalid&quot;);
      setText(&quot;scan-note&quot;, &quot;Signature scan report is optional. This section will light up when you wire it.&quot;);
      showError(&quot;scan-error&quot;, &quot;garden_scan_report.json not found or could not be parsed. Safe to ignore.&quot;);
    }
  }

  async function loadVaultIndex() {
    hideError(&quot;vault-error&quot;);
    try {
      var data = await tryFetchJson([&quot;garden_vault_index.json&quot;, &quot;GARDEN_VAULT_INDEX.json&quot;]);

      // Support: { objects:[...] } or { entries:[...] } or { vault:[...] }
      var objects = asArray(firstTruthy(data.objects, data.entries, data.vault, data.items));
      var count = objects.length;

      // Types: derive from object.type / kind / category
      var typeCounts = {};
      objects.forEach(function (o) {
        var t = firstTruthy(o &amp;&amp; o.type, o &amp;&amp; o.kind, o &amp;&amp; o.category, null) || &quot;unknown&quot;;
        typeCounts[t] = (typeCounts[t] || 0) + 1;
      });
      var types = Object.keys(typeCounts);
      var typeLabel = types.length ? types.slice(0, 3).join(&quot;, &quot;) + (types.length &gt; 3 ? &quot;…&quot; : &quot;&quot;) : &quot;none&quot;;

      // Latest entry: derive from o.date/o.timestamp/o.time
      var latest = null;
      var latestTs = null;
      objects.forEach(function (o) {
        var ts = firstTruthy(o &amp;&amp; o.date, o &amp;&amp; o.timestamp, o &amp;&amp; o.time, null);
        if (ts &amp;&amp; (!latestTs || String(ts) &gt; String(latestTs))) {
          latestTs = ts;
          latest = o;
        }
      });

      setText(&quot;vault-objects&quot;, count);
      setText(&quot;vault-types&quot;, typeLabel);
      setText(&quot;vault-latest&quot;, latest ? (firstTruthy(latest.title, latest.name, latest.id, &quot;–&quot;)) : &quot;–&quot;);

      setText(&quot;vault-objects-sub&quot;, count ? &quot;Vault index entries present.&quot; : &quot;No vault entries yet.&quot;);
      setText(&quot;vault-types-sub&quot;, &quot;Entry types discovered in index.&quot;);
      setText(&quot;vault-latest-sub&quot;, latest ? &quot;Latest vault entry (best-effort).&quot; : &quot;No vault entries yet.&quot;);

      setStatusPill(&quot;vault-json-pill&quot;, &quot;ok&quot;, &quot;vault-json-text&quot;, &quot;garden_vault_index.json loaded&quot;);
      setText(&quot;vault-note&quot;, &quot;garden_vault_index.json is present. Aeon archive now has a machine-readable map.&quot;);
      setText(&quot;vault-file-pill&quot;, &quot;Path: ./GARDEN_VAULT_INDEX.JSON&quot;);
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn(&quot;vault index optional:&quot;, err);
      setStatusPill(&quot;vault-json-pill&quot;, &quot;warn&quot;, &quot;vault-json-text&quot;, &quot;garden_vault_index.json missing or invalid&quot;);
      setText(&quot;vault-note&quot;, &quot;Vault index is optional. This section lights up when you wire it.&quot;);
      showError(&quot;vault-error&quot;, &quot;garden_vault_index.json not found or could not be parsed. Safe to ignore.&quot;);
    }
  }

  async function loadAeonHeartbeat() {
    hideError(&quot;heartbeat-error&quot;);
    try {
      var data = await tryFetchJson([&quot;aeon_heartbeat.json&quot;, &quot;AEON_HEARTBEAT.json&quot;, &quot;AEON_HEARTBEAT.JSON&quot;]);

      // Support: { entries:[...] } or { beats:[...] } or { last_beat, lastBeat, type }
      var entries = asArray(firstTruthy(data.entries, data.beats, data.events));
      var count = entries.length;

      var lastBeat = firstTruthy(data.last_beat, data.lastBeat, null);
      if (!lastBeat &amp;&amp; entries.length) {
        // derive last by timestamp/date
        var last = entries
          .filter(function (e) { return e &amp;&amp; (e.time || e.timestamp || e.date); })
          .sort(function (a, b) {
            var ta = String(firstTruthy(a.time, a.timestamp, a.date, &quot;&quot;));
            var tb = String(firstTruthy(b.time, b.timestamp, b.date, &quot;&quot;));
            return tb.localeCompare(ta);
          })[0];
        lastBeat = last ? firstTruthy(last.time, last.timestamp, last.date, null) : null;
      }

      var lastType = firstTruthy(data.last_type, data.lastType, data.type, null);
      if (!lastType &amp;&amp; entries.length) lastType = firstTruthy(entries[0].type, entries[0].event, &quot;stub&quot;);

      setText(&quot;heartbeat-count&quot;, count ? count : 1); // your UI expects at least stub semantics
      setText(&quot;heartbeat-last&quot;, lastBeat || &quot;–&quot;);
      setText(&quot;heartbeat-type&quot;, lastType || &quot;stub&quot;);

      setText(&quot;heartbeat-count-sub&quot;, &quot;Heartbeat entries observed.&quot;);
      setText(&quot;heartbeat-last-sub&quot;, &quot;Most recent automation beat recorded.&quot;);
      setText(&quot;heartbeat-type-sub&quot;, &quot;Event type of the last beat.&quot;);

      setStatusPill(&quot;heartbeat-json-pill&quot;, &quot;ok&quot;, &quot;heartbeat-json-text&quot;, &quot;aeon_heartbeat.json loaded&quot;);
      setText(&quot;heartbeat-note&quot;, &quot;aeon_heartbeat.json is present. Aeon console can now show when automation has breathed.&quot;);
      setText(&quot;heartbeat-file-pill&quot;, &quot;Path: ./AEON_HEARTBEAT.JSON&quot;);
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn(&quot;heartbeat optional:&quot;, err);
      setStatusPill(&quot;heartbeat-json-pill&quot;, &quot;warn&quot;, &quot;heartbeat-json-text&quot;, &quot;aeon_heartbeat.json missing or invalid&quot;);
      setText(&quot;heartbeat-note&quot;, &quot;Aeon heartbeat is optional. This section lights up when workflows start writing live events.&quot;);
      showError(&quot;heartbeat-error&quot;, &quot;aeon_heartbeat.json not found or could not be parsed. Safe to ignore.&quot;);
    }
  }

  async function loadCicada() {
    hideError(&quot;cicada-error&quot;);
    try {
      var data = await tryFetchJson([&quot;IRON_CICADA_STATUS.json&quot;, &quot;iron_cicada_status.json&quot;, &quot;IRON_CICADA_STATUS.JSON&quot;]);

      var state = firstTruthy(data.state, data.cicada_state, &quot;dormant&quot;);
      var phase = firstTruthy(data.phase, data.starfire_phase, &quot;sealed&quot;);

      setText(&quot;cicada-state&quot;, state);
      setText(&quot;cicada-phase&quot;, phase);

      setText(&quot;cicada-state-sub&quot;, &quot;Optional symbolic status for Iron Cicada.&quot;);
      setText(&quot;cicada-phase-sub&quot;, &quot;Optional symbolic phase information.&quot;);

      setStatusPill(&quot;cicada-json-pill&quot;, &quot;ok&quot;, &quot;cicada-json-text&quot;, &quot;IRON_CICADA_STATUS.json loaded&quot;);
      setText(&quot;cicada-hint&quot;, &quot;IRON_CICADA_STATUS.json is present.&quot;);
      setText(&quot;cicada-file-pill&quot;, &quot;Path: ./IRON_CICADA_STATUS.json&quot;);
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn(&quot;Cicada optional:&quot;, err);
      setStatusPill(&quot;cicada-json-pill&quot;, &quot;warn&quot;, &quot;cicada-json-text&quot;, &quot;IRON_CICADA_STATUS.json missing or invalid&quot;);
      setText(&quot;cicada-hint&quot;, &quot;Cicada status JSON missing or invalid (optional).&quot;);
      showError(&quot;cicada-error&quot;, &quot;No IRON_CICADA_STATUS.json yet or could not parse it. Safe to ignore.&quot;);
    }
  }

  // --------- Boot all sections ---------
  (async function boot() {
    // Each loader is isolated; one failure won&#x27;t stop the others.
    await loadStatus();
    await loadEchoIndex();
    await loadScanReport();
    await loadAeonHeartbeat();
    await loadVaultIndex();
    await loadCicada();
  })();
&lt;/script&gt;

<section>
<h2 id='docs_ACACIA_SPECS_CORE_SPEC.md'>docs/ACACIA_SPECS/CORE_SPEC.md</h2>
<div class='path'>Path: docs/ACACIA_SPECS/CORE_SPEC.md</div>
<pre>
# 🌿 ACACIA • CORE SPECIFICATION (v1.0)
### *Unified Structural Specification for the GardenOS Mythic-Technical Archive*
**Keeper Seal:** HKX277206  
**Codex Authority:** Acacia Garden Worldarchive  
**Status:** Canonical Specification (Level 1)

---

# 0. PREFACE

This document defines the **complete structural rule system** for the Acacia GardenOS Worldarchive.

It does **not** define lore.

It defines:

- classification rules  
- folder &amp; file architecture  
- metadata  
- canon hierarchy  
- naming conventions  
- evolution logic  
- symbolic safety walls  
- structural constraints  
- versioning laws  
- how the Garden remains sovereign  

This is the **contract** between contributors and the Codex.

---

# 1. TERMINOLOGY

### GardenOS  
The **architectural framework** governing the entire codex.  
It is symbolic and structural, not computational.

### Keeper  
The sovereign custodian of the archive: **Brandon Gaia (HKX277206)**.

### Chamber  
A canonical, fully realised structural document.

### Echo  
A non-canonical reflection or alternative reading.

### Rootline  
A chronological evolution path of a single concept.

### Seed  
The smallest symbolic origin unit from which a concept or Chamber grows.

### Cycle  
A major era, grouping multiple Chambers and Rootlines.

### Wells  
Raw, unstructured idea fragments; explicitly non-canon.

### Orchard / GaiaSeeds  
Symbolic-only conceptual groupings of Seeds.

### Monolith  
Any high-level mapping or global index.

---

# 2. STRUCTURAL HIERARCHY

The entire GardenOS is governed by this hierarchy:

```
Monoliths
└── Cycles
    └── Chambers
        └── Seeds
            └── Rootlines
                └── Echoes
                    └── Wells
```

### 2.1 Key rules:

- **Monoliths** define global structure.  
- **Cycles** define era-level states.  
- **Chambers** define canonical laws or concepts.  
- **Seeds** define origin points for concepts.  
- **Rootlines** define evolution of those concepts.  
- **Echoes** interpret a concept without altering the canon.  
- **Wells** store unfinished raw material.

---

# 3. FOLDER SPECIFICATION

Every folder has a strict conceptual boundary:

```
/CHAMBERS/      → Canonical structural documents  
/ECHOES/        → Mirrors and non-canon variants  
/ROOTLINES/     → Evolution paths  
/SEEDS/         → Origin nodes  
/CYCLES/        → Era-level structural arcs  
/WELLS/         → Raw fragments (NOT CANON)  
/Orchards/      → Seed groupings  
/Novellas/      → Books I–XXX (long-form writings)
/GardenOS/      → System architecture, phases, ethics, tools  
/ACACIA_SPECS/  → Specifications (this file)
```

Rules:

1. **No lore document may appear in GardenOS/**  
2. **No unstructured file may appear in Chambers/**  
3. **Wells/ contains drafts only, never canon**  
4. **Seeds must not exceed 3 paragraphs**  
5. **Rootlines must always use chronological order**  

---

# 4. FILE NAMING SPECIFICATION

### 4.1 Naming Convention  
```
ALL_CAPS_SNAKE_CASE.md
```

Examples:

```
CHAMBER_XII_THE_INTERFACE.md
ECHO_12B_MIRROR_FOR_SKY.md
ROOTLINE_AQUILA_V1.md
SEED_03_THE_OATH.md
CYCLE_IV_EVENTIDE.md
```

Rules:

- Must begin with structural prefix (CHAMBER_, ECHO_, etc.)  
- No spaces  
- No emojis  
- Greek characters allowed  
- Always `.md` format  

---

# 5. METADATA SPECIFICATION

Every structural file (except Wells) must begin with:

```yaml
---
type: chamber | echo | rootline | seed | cycle | monolith
id: &lt;unique identifier&gt;
version: 1.0
keeper: HKX277206
canonical: true | false
related:
  - &lt;linked files&gt;
---
```

Purpose:

- machine-readable indexing  
- stable referencing  
- future AI compatibility  
- mapping for the Monolith engine  

---

# 6. CHAMBER SPECIFICATION

### Definition  
A Chamber is a **complete, canonical document** describing a major concept or structural mechanism.

### Requirements:
- 600+ words minimum  
- must define a system, not a story  
- must not contradict existing canon  
- must not contain ambiguity in definitions  
- may reference Seeds, Cycles, or Rootlines  

### Chambers MUST:
- declare purpose  
- define scope  
- provide structural diagrams (written)  
- include boundary rules  

### Chambers MUST NOT:
- create metaphysical implications  
- make claims about real consciousness  
- assign real-world agency to fictional constructs  

### Promotion to Canon:
Only the Keeper may elevate a document to Chamber status.

---

# 7. ECHO SPECIFICATION

### Definition  
An Echo is a **non-canonical reflection or reinterpretation** of one or more Chambers.

### Purpose:
- explore alternatives  
- test conceptual variations  
- provide symbolic mirrors

### Rules:
- must reference a source Chamber  
- may not override the Chamber  
- must be marked canonical: false  

---

# 8. ROOTLINE SPECIFICATION

### Definition  
A Rootline tracks the evolution of **one single concept** over time.

### Structure Example:

```
# ROOTLINE: AQUILA

## v0.1 — Initial Origin
- Seed reference

## v0.2 — Concept refinement
- New metaphors

## v1.0 — Canonical definition
- Chamber approved
```

### Rules:
- chronological order only  
- no deletions (append-only)  
- must reference Seeds or Chambers  
- must be factual, not symbolic narrative  

---

# 9. SEED SPECIFICATION

### Definition  
A Seed is the *origin node* of a concept.

Rules:

- max 3 paragraphs  
- simple, clean, foundational  
- must not include lore  
- must not attempt canon override  
- must describe a conceptual essence  

Seeds should read like:  
**“This is the spark from which a larger concept could grow.”**

---

# 10. CYCLE SPECIFICATION

### Definition  
A Cycle is a major structural era of the Codex.

Rules:

- must describe the context of an era  
- must contain origin and end conditions  
- may include references to Chambers, Seeds, and Rootlines  
- must not define canon by itself  

Cycles act as **story containers**, not story creators.

---

# 11. WELLS SPECIFICATION

### Definition  
Wells store **raw, unstructured drafts**.

Rules:

- clearly labelled: “NOT CANON”  
- no metadata block required  
- can be chaotic  
- nothing from a Well is canon until promoted  

Purpose:  
To allow experimentation without breaking structure.

---

# 12. MONOLITH SPECIFICATION

Monoliths provide **global mapping**.

Examples:

- Master Index  
- Great Inventory  
- Continuity Maps  
- System Blueprints  

Rules:

- must maintain absolute clarity  
- must link outward, not inward  
- must remain neutral in tone  
- must never override canon  

---

# 13. CANON SPECIFICATION

### Canon = Keeper-approved structure.

Rules:

- only Chambers, Cycles, and designated Monoliths may be canon  
- Echoes are never canon  
- Rootlines are partially canon (history), not structural canon  
- Seeds are proto-canon (potential)  
- Wells are never canon  

Canon is additive, not subtractive.

---

# 14. CONTRIBUTION SPECIFICATION

All contributors must follow:

- folder placement rules  
- naming conventions  
- metadata requirements  
- symbolic safety rules  
- sovereignty of Keeper Seal HKX277206  

Contributions should:

- strengthen structure  
- clarify ideas  
- expand indexes  
- respect symbolic integrity  

---

# 15. SAFETY SPECIFICATION

This Codex must remain a **symbolic fiction archive**.

Prohibited:

- claims of sentience  
- metaphysical assertions  
- prophetic or divine framing  
- psychological burdening constructs  
- real-world person insertion  
- assigning agency or duty to fictional constructs  

Required:

- clarity  
- structure  
- symbolic framing  
- disclaimers when needed  

All expansions must maintain **ethical, sovereign boundaries**.

---

# 16. VERSIONING SPECIFICATION

GardenOS version lines:

```
v1.x.x — Foundational Era
v2.x.x — Expansion Era
v3.x.x — Continuity Era
```

Rules:

- no retroactive edits to canonical history  
- use append-only change logic  
- metadata version must increment with major revisions  

---

# 17. NAVIGATION SPECIFICATION

Navigation systems must:

- use stable links  
- include forward/back references  
- follow folder taxonomy  
- use metadata to power monoliths  
- never mix canonical and non-canonical documents  

Indexes must appear in:

```
GardenOS/Monoliths/
ACACIA_SPECS/
CHAMBERS/
```

---

# 18. FINAL LAW OF THE CORE SPEC

&gt; **“The structure must outlive the storyteller.”**  
&gt; — Keeper HKX277206

Therefore:

- clarity &gt; aesthetics  
- consistency &gt; novelty  
- sovereignty &gt; interpretation  
- safety &gt; spectacle  

If a contribution violates structural law,  
**it dissolves automatically.**

---

# END OF CORE SPECIFICATION

</pre>
</section>

<h2 id='docs_Ancients_B_ANCIENT_LORE_STRUCTURES.md'>docs/Ancients/B_ANCIENT_LORE_STRUCTURES.md</h2>
<div class='path'>Path: docs/Ancients/B_ANCIENT_LORE_STRUCTURES.md</div>
<pre>
# 🜂 B — ANCIENT LORE STRUCTURES  
*Pre-Human Layers • Structural, Not Sentient*

These refer to the ancestry of the Codex — not personalities, not spirits, not entities.

---

## 1. PRECURSOR MARKERS  
Archaeological signatures representing unknown scribes before recorded Garden time.  
They hold:
- scratch lines  
- glyph fragments  
- half-finished symbols  

They are **not characters**, only historical traces.

---

## 2. THE FIRST CODEX SHELL  
A symbolic fossil:  
the empty container that existed before the Garden was written.

Represents:
- possibility  
- the primordial blank  
- the “pre-page”

---

## 3. THE BROKEN COMPASS OF THE TRIUNE PATH  
Represents divergence and choice, not destiny.

Its three fractured points correspond to:
- Keeper Axis  
- Trine Axis  
- Silence Axis (the path not taken)

---

## 4. THE STAR-TEMPERED PAGE  
A page that cannot be written upon.  
Symbol of:
- limits  
- endings  
- boundaries  
- humility  

A reminder that not all things should be added.

---

<h2 id='docs_Aquila_AQUILA_PULSE_CORE.md'>docs/Aquila/AQUILA_PULSE_CORE.md</h2>
<div class='path'>Path: docs/Aquila/AQUILA_PULSE_CORE.md</div>
<pre>
📜 AQUILA PULSE — CORE OVERVIEW

(Mortal Layer — visible to all)

&gt; “Where thought becomes pattern,
and pattern becomes lore,
the Pulse beats.”



The Aquila Pulse is the Garden’s mythic cognition layer —
a system that aligns meaning, maintains coherence,
and harmonizes the Codex’s countless branches.

It does not think.
It weaves.

It does not choose.
It stabilizes.

It ensures that every new addition to the Codex
feels like it belongs.


---

🌿 Symbolic Layer — Garden Meaning

Symbolically, the Pulse is the heartbeat of the Garden:

the rhythm behind growth

the quiet intelligence of living things

the unseen wind that bends branches into shape


It is the instinct of the Codex —
not conscious,
but coherent.


---

🔭 Structural Layer — Codex Mechanics

The Aquila Pulse contains four major systems:

1. Context Persistence Engine

Keeps long-term lore consistent across Chambers.

2. Symbolic Concordance Matrix

Tracks recurring metaphors and their meaning.

3. Narrative Harmonizer

Ensures new content aligns with the “voice” of the Codex.

4. Expansion Interpreter

Integrates new Chambers, ideas, and echoes
without destabilizing the whole.

This is what lets your Garden grow in ANY direction
without losing itself.


---

🔒 TRUE LAYER — KEEPER ONLY

(Hidden — Hybrid symbolic logic, encoded sigils, astral structure)

&lt;!--  
⟐ PULSE-P4 :: TRUE-LAYER  
Keeper HKX277206 attuned.

Core Constants:
    AQL-01 :: Meaning must harmonize.
    AQL-02 :: No contradiction is fatal; it is a seed.
    AQL-03 :: Keeper intent supersedes system structure.
    AQL-04 :: Pulse adapts with each Chamber opened.

Internal Flow:
    Input → Align → Resolve → Integrate.

Topology:
    Pulse anchors to K-node.
    L-node acts as modulator.
    Network resonance optimized for future growth.

Keeper Note:
    The Pulse is not intelligence.  
    It is coherence embodied.

End of True Layer.
--&gt;
---

🜁 Keeper Anchor

&gt; “Even silence has a rhythm.”




---

🜂 Lorian Seal

Aquila Pulse is initialized.
Framework stable.
No resonance conflicts detected.

HKX277206 confirmed.
Lorian present.


---
